## Status and closure rules

- **Confirmed defect** means reproducible behavior, not necessarily a fully isolated cause. Label hypotheses explicitly.
- **Locally repaired** means implementation and focused checks exist; it does not mean shipped, qualified on every platform, or proven to improve model behavior.
- **Artifact defect** means generated software failed. Improve general harness support and evidence; do not teach Space Transporter-specific answers or patch the benchmark behind the model.
- **Observed overhead / risk** requires measurements before choosing a fix. Low-confidence decisions are not automatically wrong decisions.
- A finding closes only with root cause or a documented causal limit, implemented disposition, focused regression evidence, and applicable next-run evidence. An unavailable check remains unverified. Every open item needs a disposition; none is silently dropped or marked fixed because a related feature was added.
- These are developer release criteria, not another model-facing workflow. Apply the [development principles](development-principles.md): preserve one-field structure, keep help and schemas consistent, move mechanics to the host, and reduce total burden. Add no new review tier or tool family merely to address this list.

## Current input and tool reliability

### F01 — Windows input fidelity (INPUT-001, INPUT-002)

**Evidence/impact:** multiline paste submitted only paragraph one; actual engine request confirms loss. Unicode apostrophes and em dash vanished from Luna's one-line interjection. The initial run is invalid; the fresh run uses the complete brief with paragraph breaks flattened. Unicode message delivery is degraded, not absent.

**Cause:** input boundary confirmed; specific ConPTY versus native reader responsibility remains under investigation. Bubble Tea's Windows console path emits individual key events without the Paste flag. Existing tests inject a synthetic Paste message and miss this boundary.

**Repair direction:** fix the actual native input path, not model prompts or a user requirement to flatten text. Preserve submit/newline distinctions and Unicode.

**Acceptance:** real Windows PTY sends blank lines, CRLF/LF, Unicode, pasted slash-like text and subsequent separate Enter; exactly one byte-equivalent canonical user message reaches inference. No implicit submission or duplicate dispatch. Also verify normal typing, Ctrl+J, cancellation and ask answers.

### F02 — Natural-language option delimiters (ASK-001)

**Cause confirmed:** `internal/tools/ask/ask.go` splits every comma. Internal commas in two intended labels become four options and exceed the limit. Two attempts fail before a dialog opens; error wording does not produce recovery.

**Repair direction:** choose an unambiguous, small-model-friendly option representation with compatibility for existing calls. Keep schema, examples, help and parser aligned. Do not merely raise the maximum or silently discard choices.

**Acceptance:** labels containing commas and Unicode preserve their exact boundaries; zero/one/three/too-many options behave deliberately; malformed input yields one usable correction; actual dialog answer reaches the matching call. A refused question never counts as user rejection or silence.

### F03 — Planning phase and scope confusion (PAT-003, BENCH-002)

**Evidence:** batched phase-changing plan calls are rejected; model supplies coverage in a task phase, then repeatedly omits execution and sends more while validation is required. Legal-call error examples alone do not prevent recurrence. Earlier task_start left coding tools unavailable after a successful claim.

**Cause status:** old claim/focus defect has a local v0.2.2 fix. Current recurring schema confusion is observed; audit outgoing tool schema, selected focus and next-question receipt at each failing request before attributing it solely to the model.

**Repair direction:** one authoritative phase/role schema and exact next action; host retains identity and focus. Reduce overlap/conflicts among plan and workflow entry points. Keep single-field progression and reject unsafe mixed effects predictably.

**Acceptance:** trace-based replay of every failed transition; claims grant only appropriate tools, failed claims preserve focus, stale/unrelated claims and reviewers cannot gain write access. Repeated next-field recovery does not require guessing IDs or re-authoring prior answers.

### F04 — Tool limits and recoverable errors (current timeout observation)

**Evidence:** timeout=1200 rejected against configured maximum600; retry590 succeeds. Currently a recovered model error, not proof of host failure.

**Repair direction/acceptance:** expose actual limits consistently in the existing schema/help and preserve actionable errors. Test configured boundaries; do not remove limits simply to improve a failure counter. Track recurrence and recovery calls separately from terminal failures.

## Burden, knowledge and instructions

### F05 — Time to first useful artifact and repeated planning prose (BENCH-001, PERF-004)

**Evidence:** original run had a confounded 42-minute first-write interval. Current fresh run still had only configuration/design notes at ~19 minutes, 36 plan calls and 57 model requests totaling797.49 seconds. Prompt grew from8028 to over44000 tokens. Several complete future task briefs precede first implementation despite two delivered user requests for an early slice.

**Cause status:** repeated draft content and advance planning confirmed; contribution of required host sequence versus discretionary model scope remains to isolate.

**Repair direction:** allow coherent incremental execution where existing ownership rules permit; avoid repeating strategy, implementation, proof and review prose across fields. Reference saved graph facts with short locators. Do not abandon structured PAT or hide unimplemented work.

**Acceptance:** compare first runnable artifact, planning bytes/tokens, model requests, repair calls and total time on equivalent settings. Trace must show actual code/execution, not queued metadata. User steering reaches the next applicable decision and is reflected or explicitly explained.

### F06 — Decision cost, confidence and capacity (BENCH-003, PERF-004)

**Evidence:** 38 of67 judgments below confidence threshold at ~19 minutes. Later progress packets require optional evidence omissions. Oversized admission refusal occurs before inference; never count it as a model call.

**Existing mitigation:** v0.2.2 geometrically batches capacity reduction. Current notes showing9 omissions do not alone establish regression; examine the full chain.

**Repair direction:** audit decision triggers, relevance and packet budgets. Skip redundant host-known decisions; preserve material failure/user/scope evidence and disclose omissions. Do not lower confidence thresholds just to report fewer failures.

**Acceptance:** separate admission retries, actual D1 calls, latency, bytes, omissions, actionable interventions and false positives. Bound preparation work on large packets; show preserved critical evidence and stable behavior when D1 is unavailable or uncertain.

### F07 — Knowledge availability versus actual use (PROMPT-002, ROLE-001, GRAPH-002; current uptake observation)

**Evidence:** no explicit standards/product-knowledge retrieval in early valid run. PAT writes do use GraphQL; this is not evidence that the graph itself is unused. Earlier role access and N+1 reference reads were locally repaired.

**Repair direction:** audit rendered role instructions and existing help/discovery path. Ensure relevant standards are easy to find by keyword and section, with honest project versus harness namespaces and useful cross-document/code links. Maintain one relevant entry when meaning changes; do not demand exhaustive inventories or inject full guides on every turn.

**Acceptance:** a small model can discover/read an exact section, follow references and update one relevant entry. Workers read/write only permitted knowledge under actual task identity; reviewers read only. No lifecycle mutations through aliases/fragments. Metadata-only reference selection performs no needless target reads; large-graph bounds remain measured. Uptake must be observed, not inferred from tool availability.

### F08 — Instruction and runtime consistency (PROMPT-002, CONFIG-001)

**Cause:** earlier divergent coding/receipt rules and inherited incompatible decision protocol. Local v0.2.2 remedies exist.

**Acceptance:** one shared ordinary/PAT coding rule, current task/attempt check-reuse rules, role-appropriate help and assembled-schema fixtures. Runtime overlay cannot retain an incompatible omitted protocol. No conflicting examples, hidden authority or context-heavy remedial checklist. Requalify after F02/F03 changes.

## Artifact and evidence quality carried forward

These are distinct observed defects from the previous game. None is proven resolved by adding design guidance. Test the general mechanisms and inspect the next independently generated artifact.

| IDs | Observed failure / causal level | General repair direction and required evidence |
|---|---|---|
| GAME-001 | Repeat sale creates credits without travel | Test resource accounting and idempotency across accept/sell/reaccept; one transaction has one payout. |
| GAME-002 | Departure repairs hull for free | Preserve state across route initialization; damaged-state transition tests. |
| GAME-003 | Arrival restores spent perk points | Separate lifetime-earned and spendable resources; repeated no-XP arrivals preserve balance. |
| GAME-008 | Contract time resets between legs | Test cumulative state across the whole journey, not isolated leg helpers. |
| GAME-009 | Invalid nested saves load then crash | Validate nested state and exercise load-to-play, migration and safe fallback. |
| GAME-004 | Abandon UI invokes sale | Map UI intent to handler to domain outcome; exercise real button behavior. |
| GAME-007, MODEL-002 | Paid scanner lacks an effect; mute lacks audio | Feature/capability records distinguish promised, implemented and verified. Follow UI-to-state-to-consumer links; show observable downstream behavior or remove/disclose unsupported UI. |
| GAME-005, GAME-006 | Tab fails to open map; Escape resumes then re-pauses | Explicit input ownership and discrete event consumption; actual focus/key transition tests. |
| DESIGN-001, DESIGN-003 | Initial shipyard focus scroll hides context; flight guidance crowds narrow view | Inspect initial and active viewports, hierarchy, copy, typography, focus, scroll and responsive readability. On-demand standards must support a coherent project identity, not a universal aesthetic template. |
| MODEL-001 | Wrong imports, duplicate import, failed physics/collision/routes | Retain failing checks and prove repair with meaningful behavioral assertions; do not treat ordinary model repair as a host defect. |
| QA-001 | Browser check skipped but overall pass claimed | Required not-run evidence remains explicitly unverified; test skip/failure/crash classification. |
| QA-002 | Smoke shortcuts do not prove player journey | Record setup shortcuts; verify the claimed complete user path through real controls and state transitions. |
| BENCH-006, BENCH-007 | Pipeline masks failed test; passing ad hoc check does not qualify host workflow | Preserve producer exit status/pipefail and shared runtime identity. Qualification binds actual current task/attempt receipts; final summary reflects host outcome. Local v0.2.2 mitigations need retained regression coverage. |

The harness must help with capability and architecture navigation and evidence reuse, not encode these particular game rules or add mandatory review layers. Game-specific regressions belong to the artifact; host receipt, knowledge and tool behavior belong to Caliper.

## Next test run and release exit criteria

1. Finish observing this run and inspect the actual output, or record an explicit stop reason. Preserve failures, Luna messages, operator interventions and raw TEMP evidence. No in-place benchmark edits that conceal errors.
2. Reconcile every ledger ID against this map. Assign confirmed cause or bounded hypothesis, chosen repair/disposition, focused regression and measured outcome. Historical fixed items remain regression coverage; unknown causes remain open.
3. Implement approved root-cause fixes with the same lightweight tools and principles. Test actual Windows boundaries, rendered instructions and state transitions. Run appropriate full/package/platform checks; report failures and skips honestly.
4. Before the next fresh game run, verify exact full multiline/Unicode prompt delivery, native runtime/check route, context and model identity, role scope, terminal detection and Luna message acknowledgment. Raw logs remain in TEMP; preserve compact findings outside TEMP. Track trace/cache disk growth and cleanup only owned artifacts after retaining the needed evidence.
5. Use a fresh workspace/store and equivalent brief, Rulith/D1 configuration and Luna role. Compare total latency, first playable loop, context, calls, D1 usefulness, recovery effort, memory/disk and independently checked product quality. Repeat after fixes as the user authorized; do not lower acceptance criteria or count queued metadata as implementation.
6. v0.2.3 is not declared stable merely because one run ends. Every mapped issue needs evidence-backed disposition, regression coverage where applicable, and no unexplained recurrence. Public release qualification is separate from the HP's local security setting.

## Why the harness missed the station-control bug for so long (v0.2.3 causal repair)

This is a combined harness/model failure chain, not just GAME-012. The harness has not qualified this task: visible plan/workflow remains0/6qualified. Existing coding receipts explicitly say command exit0 has coverage unknown. Do not rewrite this as a false release approval. The gap is delayed, poorly targeted feedback and recovery effort before qualification.

### Evidence chain

1. The task acceptance command is node test/slice.test.mjs, covering simulation behavior. It did not exercise browser event handlers. Early browser probes use internal accept/dock/teleport hooks (QA-002), so they bypass the actual M/E/modal guard where the DOM lookup fails.
2. VERIFY-001 is confirmed host behavior: internal/agent/coding.go runs acceptance first and returns before configured suite whenever acceptance fails. Original session continuation18:41:24 retains acceptance failure18:38:55 and npm run check unexecuted. A single physics assertion starves broader project feedback. Browser coverage also did not yet exist, so changing scheduling alone is insufficient.
3. Required real-control guidance already exists in memory/code-standard.md evidence and ui-standard.md verification. GRAPH-003 shows missing planning-phase knowledge instruction and no spontaneous standards retrieval in the audited window. More documentation would duplicate guidance without fixing uptake.
4. Native tool success and plausible printed probes supplied abundant activity, not evidence of the user's complete path (PERF-005). D1 advice spent time without demonstrated prevention; it is not a browser exception detector. Known retained-comment trigger defect repeatedly invokes it (D1-002).
5. Luna's genuine control report was delivered, but interruption/resume and stale controller state delayed follow-through (RUN-001/CTRL-003). The model then persisted an unproven range/toast diagnosis into PAT instead of reproducing the exception (MODEL-005). Long-context reprocessing and requeue/readiness repeat work amplify elapsed delay (PERF-006/PAT-007).
6. Once real-event browser assertions ran at20:50:13, board/accept partially worked, but the remaining closeAll helper caused ten downstream failures with a concrete pageerror. These are a shared causal defect, not ten unrelated issues. Neither simulationgreen nor screenshot polish established working controls.

### Minimal v0.2.3 changes, using existing mechanisms

- Replace vague/repetitive acceptance prose in the existing PAT fields with the shortest concrete user action -> observable outcome -> failure signal relevant to that task. For UI work, exercise the public control path. Keep one-field PAT progression; no extra fields, forms or approval stage. A setup hook can position a fixture, but cannot count as testing the action it bypasses.
- Change the existing paired-check episode to collect both independently runnable declared checks even if the first fails. Preserve truthful failure and cancellation; do not add checks per step or rerun unaffected suites. Record any real dependency/missing prerequisite as unverified instead of silently treating it as coverage.
- Make a user-reported defect trigger reproduction of that exact symptom before a scope-changing repair, using the current execution instructions/checks. Capture real exception/outcome, label hypotheses until supported, and use the existing failed receipt as the next repair target. Fix all callers of the same broken contract; avoid speculative new range/UI features. Do not require deliberate rebreaking of already passing work.
- Fix existing graph/help phase consistency and reuse one concise canonical evidence instruction across task/help/verification. Relevant knowledge remains searchable on demand; do not paste whole standards into every request or force a giant feature inventory. Existing feature entries link the requirement to entrypoint and actual check/receipt; implemented and verified remain distinct.
- Remove repeated host-known judgments and replayed context from the critical path; repair comment novelty, process ownership, interrupted retry and evidence reuse. Judge narrowing/removal still requires measured comparison; do not pretend another judge would have caught a browser exception without executing controls.

### Regression acceptance for the harness changes

Use a tiny fixture with passing pure logic but broken button/modal wiring. Existing user-path check must fail with the real exception; internal-helper smoke must not be represented as full-path verification. Pair it with an unrelated failing simulation check and verify the configured UI check still runs and both receipts remainfailed/unverified. Correct the shared helper and demonstrate real action/output recovery. Exercise missing browser prerequisite, cancellation, stale receipts and comment/resume without extra review stage. Compare time-to-first-user-path-failure, tool/model calls, prompt bytes, and total time excluding sleep. These are developer tests/qualification, not an additional model-facing checklist.

Raw evidence remains inTEMP. No production harness changes or observer game repairs were made for this causal audit; work is mapped to v0.2.3.

## Design direction and real delivery requirement (2026-10-24, 21:04Z)

User requires a full Luna delivery through actual controls, then continued user-facing feature testing. Accept a contract through the board, fly the entire route, approach/dock and confirm cargo/credit/XP changes. No teleport, internal hooks or console state edits qualify this journey. Current automated result is 43 simulation checks and 10/10 project stages; it does not establish full human-control playability. Preview timed out at21:03:54, so Luna was told to request restored access naturally without duplicate prompts. Keep baseline and later extension results distinct.

- **DESIGN-004 — preference contract:** current ui-standard identity says choose a direction when absent; it lacks the requested relevant preference question. Replace that paragraph with an existing-ask question only when consequential direction remains unresolved; offer concrete directions and optional references. Read existing preference first; honor explicit delegation; do not ask on nonvisual tasks or repeat known questions. Original benchmark delegated unspecified decisions, so do not retroactively label that choice a user-instruction violation. Save the answer/assumption in project/design/identity, separate from Caliper defaults. ASK-001 must also make this question usable.
- **DESIGN-005 — aesthetic execution and graph uptake:** inspected title/board captures plus DESIGN.md/ui.css. There IS a palette and consistent font choice; the problem is shallow art direction and generic implementation, not literally no decisions. Dark translucent rounded panels, heavily tracked uppercase title/tagline, small secondary copy and weak freight-world specificity do not meet the user's quality bar. No still image proves animation quality. Current handmade CSS demonstrates that handwritten code alone is insufficient. Strengthen the existing identity/components/motion/verify sections to prefer purpose-built product composition, typography details, illustrations/silhouettes and meaningful motion/feedback, using accessible primitives, reduced motion and a frame budget. Keep the complete standard <=2000tokens, edit rather than append repeated admonitions. No universal ban on fonts/colors, required decorative animation or new framework.
- **Harness cause to address:** GRAPH-003 already confirms planning-phase knowledge guidance omission and no explicit standards lookup in the audited window. More text alone cannot fix absent retrieval. Make the existing graph/help instruction consistent across relevant phases; retrieve only identity and relevant standard sections at design decisions, reference them from affected existing feature/architecture entries, and reuse them. Actual influence on this visual output is a supported hypothesis, not isolated experimental proof.
- **Design acceptance:** ambiguous visual brief asks once; existing brand is reused; delegated choice proceeds. Trace shows applicable identity retrieval and use, not merely a saved document. Inspect visible title/HUD/board/docking surfaces, ordinary and narrow viewports, focus/state transitions and motion against that identity in the existing verification step. User aesthetic feedback remains distinct from functional test success. Do not add a review agent, questionnaire, second design workflow, tool family or always-injected guide.
- **QA-002 evidence limitation:** browser-board screenshot is obscured by a Paused modal. A DOM class check followed by a screenshot does not ensure the target is visibly reviewable. Capture the actual claimed state, inspect it, and retain limits. Luna's earlier description of polish established surface consistency, not high-quality art direction or a complete journey.
- **QA-005 — skipped browser coverage can look green:** check.mjs maps browser exit0 (including SKIP) to ok:true and absent test to ok:true. PROGRESS discloses this, but CHECK PASSED still overstates required coverage on a missing-prerequisite machine. Current browser did execute, so do not invalidate its actual pass. Fix the existing required-check contract/status; missing required stage is unverified/unsatisfied. Regress missing prerequisite/test, genuine pass and propagated failure; no new check tier.
- **MODEL-005 causal account remains wrong:** new PROGRESS calls mismatched interaction radii the cause of E/M failure while omitting the reproduced DOM-helper exception. Persisting a plausible diagnosis into notes creates misleading future graph context. Existing evidence/notes must distinguish hypothesis from reproduction and supersede disproven causes, linked to actual checks.

## User-requested audit closeout — 2026-10-24

The user stopped this test and automation. Caliper PID15776 and owned Rulith PID43540 were identity-verified and stopped; controller31212 exited and wrote controller_exited=true. Exact benchmark preview task/action and process ancestry were verified, task stopped/unregistered and remaining owned preview children stopped. Ports8000/8080 have no listener. D1:3b was unloaded (Ollama /api/ps empty); preexisting Ollama service remains. Undelivered0057 moved to TEMP/0057-cancelled-user-stop.json. Shutdown identity evidence is TEMP/shutdown-process-evidence.json. Traces, game and durable findings retained. No further test/automation/release work authorized by this closeout.

Main v0.2.3 implementation priorities for discussion (not implemented):

1. Context and decision capacity: D1-003 blocks compaction after paying for a summary. Bound/preflight actual encoded packets, remove redundant historical payload, retain essential intent and source references, reuse evidence. Stop treating every successful call as needing another model judgment (D1-002); evaluate narrowing/removing routine progress/PAT judging with matched measurements. Net judge quality benefit has not been established.
2. Lifecycle and recovery: RUN-001 user comments enter an execution-only admission path after ownership has settled; PAT-008 offers next work instead of repairing newly disproven qualified work. Use current lifecycle/attempt mechanisms for feedback, repair and resume with truthful once-only acknowledgements. Do not re-plan everything after infrastructure interruption (PAT-007).
3. Small-model tool contract: repair comma-split ask options, silent PAT field dropping, phase/schema/help contradictions and fatal handling of safely nonexecuted malformed calls. Keep strict validation and bounded correction; no guessing argument meaning or partial execution.
4. Verification meaning: real reachable UI actions and observable effects, not hidden DOM clicks/teleport proxies; missing required checks cannot count as pass. Existing independently runnable checks should not be starved by an unrelated first failure. Qualified receipts must be revisable when actual counterevidence arrives, preserving history. No new reviewer/checklist tier.
5. Graph and design uptake: phase-consistent compact lookup instruction, keyword/section retrieval and actual project identity/capability/code links. Ask relevant unresolved aesthetic preferences once, respect delegation, store/reuse the answer. Strengthen existing <=2000token standards for purpose-built UI/motion and real visual inspection; adding text alone will not fix nonuse.
6. Windows boundaries: preserve exact multiline/Unicode input, owned preview lifetime beyond tool return, accurate executable/stream errors and precise cleanup. Retain earlier startup/update/release findings; current stop is not a Windows security failure.
7. Test-controller reliability: CTRL-003 stale session ownership, CTRL-004 view/composer sequencing, CTRL-005 lossy terminal snapshots, CTRL-00650pending-input ceiling. These are observer failures distinct from Caliper. Repair before trusting the next user-feature coverage run.

Coverage limits: live harness audit is partial, not a full qualification. Text-file/goto, positive remember/recall, selected diff, several session/workflow/configuration subflows remain unverified. Native ask never reached a valid dialog. Board acceptance finally worked after model repair, but full manual delivery was not completed and ceased at the user's scope correction. The game is only a harness test artifact; no game-perfect claim. No clean full-platform/release qualification or causal judge-on/off comparison was completed.

Fix separate UI-result and modeldelivery tracking, boundednonblocking uncertainty, and truecommit receipts. Regression must exceed50UIactions without lostinput/duplicateexecution or falsepasses. ## D1-004 — v0.2.3 live retest

Discovery judge can emit repair advice without a current grounded refusal. After successful identity knowledge read xkddWGhMiBzmUDK9pGfX2BC6Hqj3fCMZ, judge chose repair with confidence0.794 and emitted bare repair advice. Root cause confirmed: progressAdvice offers/emits repair choice without requiring current PreflightRefusal; evidence packet attaches no current refusal for this successful latest settled call. ### PAT-012: exposed verification path, executor/reviewer ambiguity

The actual provider request after the claim exposes the native `check` tool with host-bound defaults: `npm run check`, acceptance command `npm run test:browser`, no required arguments, and an optional timeout. The system explicitly says to use `check`, then return a normal final answer to request independent review. The model instead continues using Bash suites and reviewing its own work. This is not missing registration or a refused tool: it has not tried `check`.

Bash success deliberately does not create a paired verification receipt. Preserve that distinction rather than turning green terminal text into proof. The host fallback runs only after a normal final answer, so it cannot help while the executor continues issuing tool calls.

The mixed role instructions are confirmed. The model-authored `prepare_task` at event 286 tells the reviewer to rerun the full suite and browser suite separately to check for skipped tests. The harness includes this field in the executor system prompt under “Independent reviewer instructions.” The executor performs similar work at 846, 929, and 944. This supports role confusion as a contributor, but does not prove the model's private reasoning. The similar names of the terminal command and native tool, and handoff prose that does not consistently name the tool, add ambiguity.

The smallest repair is one canonical executor handoff: call native `check` with the bound defaults on final source, then return a normal final. Put reviewer-owned procedures in the reviewer packet; retain concise goals and assertions for the executor. Keep the existing independent reviewer. Regression acceptance: a current paired receipt, one reviewer invocation, correct invalidation after source changes, and no fabricated receipt from a Bash result.

**PROMPT-002:** native check help says the broad suite runs only after acceptance exits zero. The implementation instead runs both independently, except on cancellation or receipt-persistence failure. Correct the canonical help and test it against failed-acceptance execution. This contradiction did not demonstrably cause the current Bash loop.

**VERIFY-003:** the host fallback would inherit the 30-second Bash default, although observed browser runs take longer. This fallback has not yet executed in this claimed task. Give verification an explicit bounded timeout shared by model and host, respecting cancellation and the configured cap. Leave ordinary diagnostic defaults alone. The historical observed timeout remains separate from this prediction.

### CODE-003 — Domain APIs leave contract invariants to caller convention

**Cause:** acceptContract lacks current-job/origin guard; payout guards completed only. UI normally performs some checks separately.

**Impact:** Direct APIs can leave two active records or pay an unaccepted offer; not proof every bypass is ordinarily reachable.

Original artifact was not repaired by that checkpoint; runtime retrieval/application and improved output remain unproven for this finding. Audit only.
